# Year diff, 2024 to 2025

Generated by `tools/year_diff.py`: the 2025 definitions' citations read against the 2024 and 2025 archived text. Of 2224 citing items, 413 are on the worklist (a document, heading or quote in one year only, or a probable value change) and 1401 more have only nearby text or figures that differ.

| --- | --- |
| no edition in one year | 104 |
| heading printed in one year only | 140 |
| quote printed in one year only | 109 |
| probable value change | 60 |
| text near the citation differs | 1302 |
| figures near the citation differ | 99 |

| parameter | 113 |
